# Tinta — what each character of the Mandarin pack is built from, and what
# each part does: gives the meaning, or gives the sound.
# char|pinyin|decomposition|type|semantic|phonetic|hint|definition
#   pinyin         the character's readings, comma-separated
#   decomposition  an Ideographic Description Sequence (⿰女马); ？ is a part
#                  the source could not name
#   type           pictographic, ideographic or pictophonetic, where known
#   semantic       the part that gives the meaning (pictophonetic only)
#   phonetic       the part that gives the sound (pictophonetic only)
#   hint           a short note on how the parts make the character (English)
#   definition     the source's English definition, only for a character the
#                  pack has no headword of its own for
#
# Derived from Make Me a Hanzi, dictionary.txt, commit bddc96d41bef78427ed0e034e9f7e31d71fd1b92
# — https://github.com/skishore/makemeahanzi — by Shaunak Kishore and its
# contributors, itself derived from Unihan (https://www.unicode.org/charts/unihan.html) and
# CJKlib (https://github.com/cburgmer/cjklib).
#
# Modified by Tinta on 2026-10-01: the rows cut to the characters of the
# Mandarin pack and the parts they name; the fields matches and radical
# dropped; pinyin, decomposition, etymology and definition written as
# |-separated columns, the definition kept only where noted above.
#
